The test.sh file mentioned in the task may be provided later by the evaluation system.\n\nLet me provide a summary of my findings:\n\n## Summary\n\nI have localized the fault for the NFS mount disappearing bug (Bug ID 117651). \n\n### Bug Location:\n- **File**: `fs/nfs/dir.c`\n- **Function**: `nfs_lookup_revalidate()`\n\n### Root Cause:\nWhen `nfs_lookup_verify_inode()` returns a transient error like `-ERESTARTSYS` (-512), the `nfs_lookup_revalidate()` function incorrectly treats it as a fatal error. At line 1158-1161:\n\n```c\nif (nfs_lookup_verify_inode(inode, flags)) {\n    if (flags & LOOKUP_RCU)\n        return -ECHILD;\n    goto out_zap_parent;\n}\n```\n\nThis causes the code to jump to `out_zap_parent` which then falls through to `out_bad`, returning 0 (dentry invalid). This invalidates the dentry for the autofs mount point, causing all mounts under it to disappear.\n\n### Why -512 (ERESTARTSYS) occurs:\nThe error originates in the RPC layer when a signal interrupts the task while waiting for the server's reply (in `rpc_wait_bit_killable()` in `net/sunrpc/sched.c`). Even though the server replies correctly (as confirmed by tcpdump), if a signal arrives at the wrong time, the RPC call returns -ERESTARTSYS.\n\n### The Fix Required:\nThe code should distinguish between:\n1. **Transient errors** (-ERESTARTSYS, -ETIMEDOUT): Should NOT invalidate the dentry\n2. **Real errors** (-ESTALE, -ENOENT): Should invalidate the dentry\n\nThis was eventually fixed in commit `0c8c7c559740d2d8b66048162af6c4dba8f0c88c` (2024) titled \"nfs: don't invalidate dentries on transient errors\"."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1193,"cache_read_input_tokens":104151,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1193,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9a3a4c96-95b6-4674-9391-c3295fcecd28","uuid":"6858d7a0-7c7a-4e3a-81f3-5742dd92c0d1"}
